Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The cosmic variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 914 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 113 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the cosmic space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 914 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 113. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in cosmic theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 541 and the invariant I(A,B) = 716 mod 3.

Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
